## 1. EDUCATION
*34 words*

| Word | Meaning | Collocation | Example |
|------|---------|-------------|---------|
| **get much out of that**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɡɛt ˈsʌmθɪŋ aʊt əv ˈsʌmθɪŋ/</span> | thu được điều gì từ việc gì đó | didn't **get much out of that** | *I didn't get much out of that.* |
| **grid paper**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɡrɪd ˈpeɪpə/</span> | giấy có ô | use **grid paper**<br>write on **grid paper** | *One quite simple thing you can do is to use grid paper.* |
| **note-taking**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈnəʊt ˌteɪkɪŋ/</span> | việc ghi chú | digital / handwritten **note-taking**<br>habits of **note-taking** | *True, but people are relying on digital devices, which changes habits like note-taking and makes their punctuation completely inconsistent, which you can put down to lack of practice.* |
| **module convener**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈmɒdjuːl kənˈviːnə/</span> | người phụ trách học phần | contact the **module convener**<br>course **module convener** | *No one else on the course is doing the same as us, although it is one of the research areas of the module convener.* |
| **visiting lecturers**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈvɪzɪtɪŋ ˈlektʃərərz/</span> | giảng viên khách mời | invite / listen to **visiting lecturers** | *What did you think about the sessions from visiting lecturers, Fergus?* |
| **External Course Registration**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪkˈstɜːrnl kɔːrs ˌredʒɪˈstreɪʃn/</span> | đăng ký khóa học bên ngoài | contact / write to **External Course Registration** | *MAN: Hello, Stainford College External Course Registration, can I help you?* |
| **fill up fast**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/fɪl ʌp fæst/</span> | nhanh chóng kín chỗ | [VP] **fill up fast**<br>classes **fill up fast** | *I got a leaflet about them in the post, but I lost it and I understand some of the classes are filling up fast, so I might need to book quite soon if I want to go ahead.* |
| **Human Geography**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈhjuːmən dʒiˈɒɡrəfi/</span> | địa lý nhân văn | study / topic for **Human Geography** | *Colin, I'm really struggling to think of a topic for our Human Geography assignment.* |
| **one-day classes**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈwʌn deɪ klɑːs/</span> | lớp học một ngày | offer / sign up for **one-day classes** | *WOMAN: Yeah, I'm ringing to find out about one-day classes next year.* |
| **Taster Days**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈteɪstə deɪ/</span> | ngày học thử | attend / book **Taster Days** | *Well, you might be interested in our Taster Days.* |
| **waiting list**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈweɪtɪŋ lɪst/</span> | danh sách chờ | join / put sb on a **waiting list** | *I'm afraid that all the places are taken at present, but I can put you on the waiting list if you want?* |
| **academic performance**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌækəˈdɛmɪk pəˈfɔːməns/</span> | thành tích học tập | impact / improve **academic performance**<br>poor / strong **academic performance** | *Marks are definitely affected if examiners can't read the script, impacting their overall academic performance.* |
| **educated**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈɛʤʊkeɪtɪd/</span> | có học thức | highly / well **educated**<br>considered **educated** | *Well, in the past, cursive writing was certainly considered more stylish and educated, but not anymore.* |
| **investigate**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪnˈvestɪɡeɪt/</span> | diều tra; nghiên cứu | [VP] **investigate** + sth<br>thoroughly / further **investigate** | *Yes, and her description of how robotics can be used to investigate threats to biodiversity.* |
| **looking into**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/lʊk ˈɪntuː/</span> | diều tra, tìm hiểu | [VP] **look** + into sth<br>start **looking into** | *I didn't know theatre programmes are called playbills in the USA till I started looking into the topic.* |
| **publicise findings**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈpʌblɪsaɪz ˈfaɪndɪŋz/</span> | công bố kết quả nghiên cứu | [VP] **publicise** + **findings**<br>widely **publicise findings** | *And the researchers had a good system for publicising their findings too.* |
| **research project**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈsɜːtʃ ˈprɒdʒekt/</span> | dự án nghiên cứu | carry out / lead a **research project**<br>impressive **research project** | *The research project she described was impressive, wasn't it?* |
| **educational**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌedʒuˈkeɪʃənl/</span> | Thuộc về giáo dục | **educational** programme \ | *MAN: And I understand you organize educational visits for school parties.* |
| **beginners**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/bɪˈɡɪnə(r)/</span> | Người mới bắt đầu | suitable for **beginners** \ | *When I first spoke to Gary on the phone, he said it was a class for beginners, but I was still worried that everyone would be better than me.* |
| **practise**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈpræktɪs/</span> | Luyện tập | regularly **practise** \ | *Then he hands out the song, and if there's a new chord in it, we practise that before we play it together, but really slowly.* |
| **concentrate**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈkɒnsəntreɪt/</span> | Tập trung | fully **concentrate** \ | *But people like it, because they can really concentrate.* |
| **residential course**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌrezɪˈdenʃl kɔːs/</span> | Khóa học nội trú | attend a **residential course** | *After a year, I did a one week call-in residential course, led by specialists.* |
| **wave tank**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈweɪv tæŋk/</span> | Bể sóng | test in a **wave tank** | *They had a wave tank, where they could create extreme weather conditions so we could get experience at what to do if the boat turned over in a storm at night for example.* |
| **workshops**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈwɜːkʃɒp/</span> | hội thảo | run **workshops** | *Just to give you an idea of what's on offer in the workshops, first of all, there's a very special event called Super Heroes.* |
| **some role-play**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈrəʊl pleɪ/</span> | hoạt động đóng vai | do a **role-play** | *...faraway lands with an opportunity for aspiring actors to do some role-play....* |
| **teenage diary**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈtiːneɪdʒ ˈdaɪəri/</span> | nhật ký tuổi teen | keep a **teenage diary** | *It's basically the teenage diary of a boy from Somalia who comes to live in the UK.* |
| **independently**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌɪndɪˈpendəntli/</span> | một cách độc lập | work **independently** | *A lot of parents give up reading aloud to their children as soon as they learn to read independently, but this is a mistake.* |
| **Librarian**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/laɪˈbreəriən/</span> | thủ thư | ask the **librarian** | *Librarian should be able to help you with this.* |
| **practical teaching**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈpræktɪkl ˈtiːtʃɪŋ/</span> | Giảng dạy thực tế | **practical teaching** experience | *How are you getting on with the practical teaching?* |
| **struggling**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈstrʌɡl/</span> | Gặp khó khăn | **struggling** students | *I'm really struggling with my Year 12 science class.* |
| **discipline problems**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈdɪsəplɪn ˈprɒbləm/</span> | Vấn đề kỷ luật | face **discipline problems** | *Claire: Well, I don't have discipline problems as such.* |
| **engagement**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪnˈɡeɪdʒmənt/</span> | Sự tham gia | student **engagement** | *They listen to what I say and I gave them a test last week and the results weren't too bad, but there's no real engagement.* |
| **spine**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/spaɪn/</span> | gáy sách | the book's **spine** | *Jane: He says you have to support the spine, otherwise the paper can come away from the cover.* |
| **cover**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈkʌvə/</span> | bìa sách | damaged **cover** | *Jane: He says you have to support the spine, otherwise the paper can come away from the cover.* |

## 2. LIFESTYLE
*69 words*

| Word | Meaning | Collocation | Example |
|------|---------|-------------|---------|
| **modern lifestyle**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈmɒdən ˈlaɪfstɑɪl/</span> | lối sống hiện đại | adopt a **modern lifestyle** | *The downside of a modern lifestyle, I guess.* |
| **pick up**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/pɪk ʌp/</span> | đến lấy; nhận | [VP] **pick** + up sth<br>go and **pick up** | *Well, I've got to go and pick up my laptop.* |
| **routine**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ruːˈtiːn/</span> | thói quen hằng ngày | disturb / follow a **routine**<br>daily **routine** | *They may also be negatively affected by having their routine disturbed.* |
| **candle making**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈkændl meɪkɪŋ/</span> | làm nến | learn / take up **candle making** | *And I think there was a class in candle making?* |
| **DIY for beginners**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌdiː aɪ ˈwaɪ fər bɪˈɡɪnərz/</span> | tự sửa chữa cơ bản cho người mới bắt đầu | take a class in **DIY for beginners** | *That was on DIY for beginners.* |
| **escape from**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪˈskeɪp frɒm/</span> | thoát khỏi | [VP] **escape** + from sth<br>a welcome / relaxing **escape from** | *...and they would also go on boat trips up and down the river just for pleasure, as a relaxing escape from the noise and bustle of the city streets.* |
| **face massage**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈfeɪs məsɑːʒ/</span> | mát-xa mặt | give / have a **face massage** | *I think there was one on face massage.* |
| **go for**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈɡəʊ fə/</span> | chọn, quyết định làm gì | [VP] **go for** + sth<br>shall we **go for** | *Now, shall we go for coffee?* |
| **walker**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈwɔːkə/</span> | người đi bộ | local **walker**<br>a **walker** found / discovered | *Here, a walker found it on the ground after a rainstorm washed away some of the earth and sand.* |
| **accommodation**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/əˌkɒməˈdeɪʃn/</span> | chỗ ở | provide / find **accommodation**<br>suitable **accommodation** | *Or accommodation for one person?* |
| **cleaning service**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈkliːnɪŋ ˈsɜːvɪs/</span> | dịch vụ dọn dẹp | provide / offer a **cleaning service**<br>professional / regular **cleaning service** | *But one helpful thing is that they also provide a cleaning service for customers.* |
| **furniture rental**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈfɜːnɪtʃə ˈrɛntl/</span> | dịch vụ cho thuê đồ nội thất | use a **furniture rental** service<br>monthly **furniture rental** | *When I viewed the house, I told you I'll most probably need to rent some furniture using a furniture rental service, at least until I know whether my temporary work contract is going to be made permanent.* |
| **household repairs**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈhaʊshəʊld rɪˈpeərz/</span> | sửa chữa trong nhà | do / learn **household repairs** | *I'd like to learn how to do household repairs.* |
| **housing**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈhaʊzɪŋ/</span> | nhà ở | provide / build **housing**<br>affordable **housing** | *We'd better decide now what type of housing we're going to focus on.* |
| **bargain**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈbɑːɡɪn/</span> | món hời | real / absolute **bargain**<br>find a **bargain** | *On some seats there's a 75% discount, which is a real bargain.* |
| **bookshops**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈbʊkʃɒp/</span> | hiệu sách | browse / visit **bookshops**<br>independent **bookshops** | *It'd be nice to have a choice of bookshops, you know.* |
| **choice**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/tʃɔɪs/</span> | sự lựa chọn | have / offer a **choice**<br>wide / good **choice** | *It'd be nice to have a choice of bookshops, you know.* |
| **collection**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/kəˈlekʃn/</span> | việc đến lấy hàng | ready for / arrange **collection**<br>prompt **collection** | *They had to order a spare part, but apparently it's ready for collection now.* |
| **extra charge**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈekstrə tʃɑːrdʒ/</span> | phụ phí | incur / pay an **extra charge** | *The actual cost is £48, but then there's an extra charge as well as that for the ingredients.* |
| **grocery chains**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈɡrəʊsəri tʃeɪn/</span> | chuỗi cửa hàng thực phẩm | shop at / major **grocery chains**<br>large **grocery chains** | *Businesses who exhibit with us (each exhibitor) can claim discounts on a number of popular brands, including major high street fashion and jewellery outlets, as well as grocery chains.* |
| **high street**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌhaɪ ˈstriːt/</span> | phố mua sắm chính | shop on / major **high street**<br>**high street** brands / stores | *Businesses who exhibit with us (each exhibitor) can claim discounts on a number of popular brands, including major high street fashion and jewellery outlets, as well as grocery chains.* |
| **natural products**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈnætʃrəl ˈprɑːdʌkts/</span> | sản phẩm tự nhiên | use / make from **natural products** | *I think one reason why people like it is because the candles are all made out of natural products.* |
| **out-of-town shopping centre**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌaʊt əv ˈtaʊn ˈʃɒpɪŋ ˈsentər/</span> | trung tâm mua sắm ngoại ô | visit / build an **out-of-town shopping centre**<br>new **out-of-town shopping centre** | *And have you been to the new out-of-town shopping centre outside Broadbeach yet, Martin?* |
| **preferred day**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/prɪˈfɜːd deɪ/</span> | ngày ưa thích | choose / have a **preferred day**<br>less busy **preferred day** | *The shops are less busy then, which is always my preferred day.* |
| **range of shops**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/reɪndʒ əv ʃɒps/</span> | sự đa dạng cửa hàng | offer / have a **range of shops**<br>wide **range of shops** | *And what do you think about the range of shops in Broadbeach?* |
| **retail centres**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈriːteɪl ˈsɛntə/</span> | trung tâm bán lẻ | new **retail centres** | *They've opened some massive new shopping centres / retail centres outside my city.* |
| **shoppers**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈʃɒpər/</span> | người mua hàng | survey / attract **shoppers**<br>local **shoppers** | *I'm doing a survey of shoppers in Broadbeach.* |
| **shopping centres**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈʃɒpɪŋ ˈsɛntə/</span> | trung tâm mua sắm | build / open **shopping centres** | *They've opened some massive new shopping centres / retail centres outside my city.* |
| **shopping habits**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈʃɒpɪŋ ˈhæbɪt/</span> | thói quen mua sắm | change / affect **shopping habits** | *Of course, passenger traffic on roads might decrease as more people start working from home, but another recent development, the huge rise in online shopping altering people's shopping habits, has meant that another form of urban traffic just keeps on growing...* |
| **worth buying**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/wɜːθ ˈbaɪɪŋ/</span> | đáng mua | definitely / not **worth buying** | *We can provide these, and I wouldn't say they are worth buying yet.* |
| **cool**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/kuːl/</span> | sành điệu, ngầu | really / so **cool**<br>**cool** image / brand | *The fact that this campaign aggravated and upset competitors producing milk from dairy cows was seen as a plus as it helped to make oat milk seem cool and appeal to modern consumers.* |
| **Cupro**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈkjuːprəʊ/</span> | vải Cupro | make from / produce **Cupro**<br>semi-synthetic **Cupro** | *Now, there are a few semi-synthetic fabrics that I'd never heard of, like Cupro[/ˈkjuːprəʊ/ : (noun) vải Cupro], for example.* |
| **hairdresser**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈheədresər/</span> | tiệm làm tóc; thợ làm tóc | go to / visit the **hairdresser**<br>local **hairdresser** | *Well, I've just been to the hairdresser.* |
| **long-lasting fabric**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/lɒŋ ˈlɑːstɪŋ ˈfæbrɪk/</span> | loại vải bền lâu | produce / create **long-lasting fabric**<br>durable **long-lasting fabric** | *And it also lasts a long time (cf. long-lasting fabric [/lɒŋ ˈlɑːstɪŋ ˈfæbrɪk/ : (noun) loại vải bền lâu]) and can be recycled.* |
| **Lyocell**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈlaɪəsel/</span> | vải Lyocell | produce / make from **Lyocell**<br>sustainable **Lyocell** | *Let's start with Lyocell[/ˈlaɪəsel/ : (noun) vải Lyocell].* |
| **moisturized**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈmɔɪstʃəraɪz/</span> | dưỡng ẩm | [VP] be **moisturized**<br>already **moisturized** | *Some actors will arrive having already cleansed and moisturized their skin.* |

| Word | Meaning | Collocation | Example |
|------|---------|-------------|---------|
| **commit an offence**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/kəˈmɪt ən əˈfɛns/</span> | phạm lỗi | [VP] **commit** + an **offence**<br>deliberately **commit an offence** | *In 1874, referees were allowed to send off players if they commit an offence.* |
| **food fraud**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈfuːd frɔːd/</span> | gian lận thực phẩm | detect / prevent **food fraud** | *Ah, food fraud, yes.* |

| **poison**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈpɔɪzn/</span> | đầu độc | [VP] **poison** + sth/sb<br>deliberately / accidentally **poison** | *Or they may poison the birds, which again is illegal and can have negative effects on the whole ecosystem.* |

| **put someone at risk**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/pʊt ˈsʌmwʌn ət rɪsk/</span> | đặt ai vào tình huống nguy hiểm | [VP] **put** + sb at **risk**<br>seriously **put** at **risk** | *To comply with this, employers cannot just provide ill-fitting gear, which can put someone at risk and hamper work, especially in the emergency services.* |
| **safety implications**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈseɪfti ˌɪmplɪˈkeɪʃən/</span> | hàm ý an toàn | have / raise **safety implications**<br>serious **safety implications** | *This has serious safety implications for all women.* |
| **safety information**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈseɪfti ɪnfəˈmeɪʃn/</span> | thông tin an toàn | provide / understand **safety information** | *He or she will make sure you understand everything you need to know about safety (cf. safety information).* |

<a id="section-9"></a>
## 9. GOVERNMENT
*16 words*

| Word | Meaning | Collocation | Example |
|------|---------|-------------|---------|
| **comply with**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/kəmˈplaɪ wɪð/</span> | tuân thủ với | [VP] **comply** + with sth<br>fully **comply with** | *To comply with this, employers cannot just provide ill-fitting gear, which can put someone at risk and hamper work, especially in the emergency services.* |
| **formal rules**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈfɔːrml ruːlz/</span> | quy định trang trọng | strict / follow **formal rules**<br>**formal rules** about sth | *And another perception is that cruises have lots of rules about what to wear and how to behave.* |
| **government policy**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈɡʌvənmənt ˈpɒləsi/</span> | chính sách chính phủ | shape / influence **government policy**<br>change **government policy** | *No, though she did point to where policy changes (cf. government policy) could be made to protect our marine and coastal environments.* |
| **impose a tax**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪmˈpoʊz ə tæks/</span> | áp thuế | [VP] **impose a tax** on sb/sth<br>decide to **impose a tax** | *Several cities, such as Barcelona, have responded by imposing a tax, which all visitors to the city from cruise ships have to pay.* |
| **imposed restrictions**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪmˈpəʊz rɪˈstrɪkʃənz/</span> | áp đặt các hạn chế | [VP] **impose** + **restrictions** on sth<br>strict / new **restrictions** | *When the government imposed restrictions on the use of paper.* |
| **legal**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈliːɡl/</span> | hợp pháp | strictly / fully **legal**<br>**legal** measures / action | *However, although these birds of prey are protected by legal measures, their numbers are falling.* |

| **invertebrates**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪnˈvɜːtɪbrət/</span> | Động vật không xương sống | tiny **invertebrates** | *For my project on invertebrates, I chose to study tardigrades.* |
| **microscopic**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌmaɪkrəˈskɒpɪk/</span> | Siêu nhỏ, chỉ có thể nhìn thấy qua kính hiển vi | **microscopic** organisms | *These are microscopic, or to be more precise near microscopic animals.* |

| **water bears**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈwɔːtə beər/</span> | Gấu nước | known as **water bears** | *They're also sometimes called water bears, water because that's where they thrive best, and bear because of the way they move.* |

| **suction**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈsʌkʃn/</span> | Lực hút | use **suction** | *And these work by means of suction.* |
| **cavity**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈkævəti/</span> | Khoang | a body **cavity** | *Instead, oxygen and also blood are transported in a fluid that fills the cavity of the body.* |

| **withstand**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/wɪðˈstænd/</span> | Chịu đựng | **withstand** extreme conditions | *And they're also able to withstand temperatures as cold as minus 200 degrees centigrade, or highs of more than 148 degrees centigrade, which is incredibly hot.* |

| **findings**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈfaɪndɪŋ/</span> | Kết quả nghiên cứu | publish the **findings** | *But the findings for any experiment are going to be limited.* |

| **follow-up**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈfɒləʊ ʌp/</span> | Tiếp theo | **follow-up** study | *Then later on, you could do a follow-up experiment, adding another variable.* |
| **variable**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈveəriəbl/</span> | Biến số, yếu tố thay đổi | control a **variable** | *Then later on, you could do a follow-up experiment, adding another variable.* |
| **genetic structure**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/dʒəˈnetɪk ˈstrʌktʃər/</span> | Cấu trúc di truyền | alter the **genetic structure** | *Claire: Or I could repeat the experiment, but change the type of food I provided, or use mice with a different genetic structure.* |
| **procedure**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/prəˈsiːdʒər/</span> | Thủ tục | follow a **procedure** | *So can I talk through a possible procedure for the experiment where mice are given a sugar supplement?* |
| **control group**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/kənˈtrəʊl ɡruːp/</span> | Nhóm đối chứng | compare with the **control group** | *Then each group would go into a separate cage and one group, let's call them group A, would be the control group.* |
| **electronic balance**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪˌlekˈtrɒnɪk ˈbæləns/</span> | Cân điện tử | weigh on an **electronic balance** | *And you'd need an electronic balance.* |
| **weighing chamber**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈweɪɪŋ ˈtʃeɪmbər/</span> | Phòng cân | seal the **weighing chamber** | *So you need something called a weighing chamber to stop the mice from running away.* |
| **standard deviation**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈstændəd ˌdiːviˈeɪʃn/</span> | Độ lệch chuẩn | calculate the **standard deviation** | *So once we've measured the weight gain of each mouse, we can work out the average for each group, as well as the standard deviation, and then see where we go from there.* |
| **genetic variation**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/dʒəˈnetɪk ˌveəriˈeɪʃn/</span> | biến thể di truyền | increase **genetic variation** | *...with appropriate levels of genetic diversity, the amount of genetic variation found within a species essential for their survival....* |

*30 words*

| Word | Meaning | Collocation | Example |
|------|---------|-------------|---------|

| **surviving document**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/səˈvaɪvɪŋ ˈdɒkjumənt/</span> | tài liệu còn tồn tại | earliest **surviving document**<br>rare **surviving document** | *It's for The Tragedy of Jane Shaw, and it's said to be the earliest surviving document to have been printed on Australia's first printing press, making it historically significant.* |
| **ancient**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈeɪʃənt/</span> | cổ đại | **ancient** civilisation / ruins | *And even if ancient pots are found in small pieces, they still provide a lot of information about the past.* |
| **animal bones**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈænɪml bəʊn/</span> | xương động vật | make tools from **animal bones**<br>find **animal bones** | *The people who once lived here were skilled at making tools from animal bones, as you'll see when you visit the exhibition.* |
| **archaeologists**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌɑːkiˈɒlədʒɪst/</span> | nhà khảo cổ học | team of **archaeologists**<br>**archaeologists** discover / excavate | *There's no doubt that pottery has given archaeologists a fascinating insight into how ancient humans lived.* |
| **brooches**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/brəʊʧ/</span> | trâm, ghim (trang sức) | find / discover **brooches**<br>ancient / decorative **brooches** | *Normally you'd expect to find brooches and other jewellery, but we're still waiting to uncover any such items.* |
| **buried**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈɛri/</span> | chôn | deeply **buried**<br>remain / lie **buried** | *Coins are often found by people using metal detectors to look for things buried in the ground, or coins are uncovered when wild animals like rabbits have been digging tunnels.* |
| **excavation**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌekskəˈveɪʃən/</span> | cuộc khai quật | carry out / conduct an **excavation**<br>archaeological **excavation** | *One area we excavated in the first summer uncovered the site of a meeting hall during our excavation.* |
| **field system**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/fiːld ˈsɪstəm/</span> | hệ thống đồng ruộng | identify / find a **field system**<br>ancient **field system** | *No other houses or huts so far, but we can see the borders of an ancient field system.* |
| **fish pond**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/fɪʃ pɒnd/</span> | ao nuôi cá | discover a **fish pond**<br>ancient **fish pond** | *Last year, we discovered a fish pond in the ancient village.* |
| **huts**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/hʌt/</span> | túp lều | build **huts**<br>ancient / wooden **huts** | *No other houses or huts so far, but we can see the borders of an ancient field system.* |
| **impressions**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪmˈprɛʃənz/</span> | dấu ấn, vết hằn | leave / make **impressions** | *The shape of an artefact may have been lost, but archaeologists can tell whether the pots were for, say, storage or cooking by examining the impressions on the clay, the scratches from tools.* |
| **post holes**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/pəʊst həʊl/</span> | hố cắm cột | find / identify **post holes**<br>deep **post holes** | *We knew it was an important building because it had two rows of post holes, deep enough to support a large roof.* |
| **remains**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/rɪˈmeɪnz/</span> | di tích, tàn tích | find / uncover the **remains**<br>ancient **remains** | *Over the three summers the team has been here, we've found the remains of several buildings, and more broken pots than you can count.* |
| **rubbish pit**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈrʌbɪʃ pɪt/</span> | hố rác | find / excavate a **rubbish pit**<br>ancient **rubbish pit** | *You might be interested to see the rubbish pit.* |
| **ruined buildings**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈruːɪnd ˈbɪldɪŋ/</span> | tòa nhà bị tàn phá | discover / find **ruined buildings**<br>ancient **ruined buildings** | *Just by chance, the team found some old maps and documents in our library, which showed 500-year-old drawings of ruined buildings on the grassy area between the outer stone walls of the castle and the river.* |
| **scratches**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈskræʧɪz/</span> | vết trầy, vết xước | deep / surface **scratches**<br>leave **scratches** | *The shape of an artefact may have been lost, but archaeologists can tell whether the pots were for, say, storage or cooking by examining the impressions on the clay, the scratches from tools.* |
| **ordinary**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈɔːdnri/</span> | bình thường | **ordinary** people / life<br>quite **ordinary** | *I also think it's important to mention that lots of ordinary people at that time were illiterate, so theatre programmes were of limited value in advertising plays.* |
| **divers**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈdaɪvə/</span> | thợ lặn | a team of **divers**<br>**divers** search / explore | *And it's really exciting today because a team of divers are in the river searching for lost objects.* |
| **metal detectors**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈmɛtl dɪˈtɛktə/</span> | máy dò kim loại | use **metal detectors**<br>search with **metal detectors** | *Coins are often found by people using metal detectors to look for things buried in the ground, or coins are uncovered when wild animals like rabbits have been digging tunnels.* |
| **uncovered**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ʌnˈkʌvə/</span> | phát hiện, khai quật | [VP] **uncover** + sth<br>recently / accidentally **uncovered** | *Coins are often found by people using metal detectors to look for things buried in the ground, or coins are uncovered when wild animals like rabbits have been digging tunnels.* |
| **Neolithic sites**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌniːəˈlɪθɪk saɪt/</span> | Di tích thời kỳ đồ đá mới | ancient **Neolithic sites** \ | *For my presentation today, I'm going to talk about the Céide Fields in the north-west of Ireland, one of the largest Neolithic sites in the world.* |
| **iron probes**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈaɪən prəʊb/</span> | Thanh sắt dùng để thăm dò | use **iron probes** \ | *He inserted iron probes into the bog to map the formation of the stones, a traditional method which local people had always used for finding fuel buried in the bog for thousands of years.* |
| **extremely well-preserved**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌwel prɪˈzɜːvd/</span> | Được bảo tồn tốt | **extremely well-preserved** remains \ | *Thanks to the bog which covers the area, the remains of the settlement at Céide Fields, which is over 5,000 years old, are extremely well-preserved.* |
| **predecessors**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈpriːdəsesər/</span> | Người đi trước, tiền thân | their **predecessors** \ | *Neolithic farmers generally lived in larger communities than their predecessors, with a number of houses built around a community building.* |
| **permanent settlements**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈpɜːmənənt ˈsetlmənt/</span> | Khu định cư vĩnh viễn | establish **permanent settlements** \ | *As they lived in permanent settlements, Neolithic farmers were able to build bigger houses.* |
| **Fragments of Neolithic pots**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈfræɡmənts əv ˌniːəˈlɪθɪk pɒts/</span> | Mảnh gốm thời kỳ đồ đá mới | discover **fragments of Neolithic pots** \ | *Fragments of Neolithic pots have been found in Céide and elsewhere in Ireland.* |
| **abandonment**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/əˈbændənmənt/</span> | Sự bỏ hoang | lead to **abandonment** \ | *The soil would have become less productive and led to the abandonment of farming.* |

| **kiln**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/kɪln/</span> | lò nung | fire something in a **kiln** | *To make pottery that will last, you need a potter's wheel, a kiln, which is basically a very hot oven where you fire the pottery, and some tools.* |

| **rhythm**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈrɪðəm/</span> | Nhịp điệu | sense of **rhythm** \ | *Here the children experiment with natural materials to create sounds and explore rhythm and tempo.* |

## 22. ECONOMICS
*21 words*

| Word | Meaning | Collocation | Example |
|------|---------|-------------|---------|
| **astronomical prices**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌæstrəˈnɒmɪkl praɪs/</span> | giá rất cao | sell at / reach **astronomical prices** | *Warehouses that were once used for storing goods are now being converted to expensive restaurants and also into apartments with river views, which are in great demand and sell for astronomical prices.* |
| **average saving**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈævərɪdʒ ˈseɪvɪŋ/</span> | mức tiết kiệm trung bình | make / calculate an **average saving**<br>significant **average saving** | *In fact, the average saving made by each exhibitor came to over £400.* |
| **budgeted for**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈbʌdʒɪt fə/</span> | dự trù ngân sách cho | [VP] **budget** + for sth<br>not **budgeted for** | *And I bet it's often not budgeted for.* |
| **charge**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ʧɑːʤ/</span> | tính phí | [VP] **charge** + sb (for sth)<br>extra / additional **charge** | *But if you chose them, you need to be aware that they charge an extra 12% every month in case of damage.* |
| **claim discounts**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/kleɪm ˈdɪskaʊnt/</span> | nhận ưu đãi giảm giá | [VP] **claim** + **discounts** on sth<br>automatically **claim discounts** | *Businesses who exhibit with us (each exhibitor) can claim discounts on a number of popular brands.* |
| **command a large fee**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/kəˈmɑːnd ə fiː/</span> | yêu cầu một mức phí | [VP] **command** + a **large** **fee**<br>can **command a large fee** | *...although there are influential influencers with hundreds of thousands of followers who can command a large fee for their services.* |
| **economic loss**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌiːkəˈnɒmɪk lɒs/</span> | tổn thất kinh tế | cause / suffer **economic loss**<br>considerable / significant **economic loss** | *Even so, these birds of prey remain a major threat to the chickens' survival, and cause considerable economic loss to farmers.* |
| **for free**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/fə friː/</span> | miễn phí | attend / get something **for free** | *It's a good idea to help at the festival you have a personal interest in because you can attend most of the events for free.* |
| **great value**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɡreɪt ˈvæljuː/</span> | rất đáng tiền | offer / represent **great value**<br>**great value** for money | *It's great value[/ɡreɪt ˈvæljuː/ : (noun) rất đáng tiền].* |
| **make a profit**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/meɪk ə ˈɒfɪt/</span> | có lợi nhuận | [VP] **make** + a **profit**<br>struggle to / fail to **make a profit** | *The owners of Basque found it was hard to make a profit[/meɪk ə ˈɒfɪt/ : (verb) có lợi nhuận] because the price of fish was so high.* |
| **mid-range price**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/mɪd reɪnʤ praɪs/</span> | mức giá tầm trung | offer a **mid-range price**<br>affordable **mid-range price** | *I'd say they're a mid company with a mid-range price.* |
| **unaffordable**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌʌnəˈfɔːdəbəl/</span> | không thể chi trả nổi | become / remain **unaffordable**<br>completely / increasingly **unaffordable** | *This plant is native to Peru and when demand peaked, the price soared, making it unaffordable for local people.* |
| **demand**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/dɪˈmɑːnd/</span> | nhu cầu | meet / increase **demand**<br>growing **demand** | *With most crops, farmers don't have to think very far ahead, so they can easily change what crop they produce, or how much of a crop they produce, if they find the demand for that crop is rising or falling.* |
| **producer to consumer**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/prəˈduːsər tuː kənˈsuːmər/</span> | từ nhà sản xuất đến người tiêu dùng | from **producer to consumer**<br>track from **producer to consumer** | *But what the researchers succeeded in doing was to ensure that these tests were carried out at different stages in the food's journey, from the producer to the consumer.* |
| **stimulate**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈstɪmjʊleɪt/</span> | kích thích | [VP] **stimulate** + sth<br>**stimulate** demand / growth | *A British PR company was hired to raise its profile and stimulate demand, also assisting local growers.* |
| **surge in demand**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/sɜːʤ ɪn dɪˈmɑːnd/</span> | sự tăng mạnh nhu cầu | cause / see a **surge in demand**<br>sudden / huge **surge in demand** | *Food trends can benefit producers, but ethical concerns have been raised about the effects a surge in demand can cause.* |
| **industrial civilisation**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪnˈdʌstriəl ˌsɪvəlaɪˈzeɪʃn/</span> | nền văn minh công nghiệp | modern / advanced **industrial civilisation** | *Much of the world now lives in an industrial civilisation, but this has only become possible because we have the necessary natural resources(cf. natural resource).* |
| **employment**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪmˈplɔɪmənt/</span> | việc làm | gain / create **employment**<br>full-time **employment** | *Her main business interest is in technology, but she's also passionate about helping people gain employment, and organises an annual jobs fair.* |
| **labour-intensive**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈleɪbər ɪnˈtensɪv/</span> | tốn nhiều lao động | highly / extremely **labour-intensive**<br>**labour-intensive** process | *It's very labour-intensive.* |
| **mobile workforce**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈməʊbaɪl ˈwɜːkfɔːs/</span> | lực lượng lao động linh động | increasingly **mobile workforce** | *A more plausible explanation is that with a mobile workforce, people are having to move around for work and often end up living miles away from their family and friends.* |
| **sustainable economy**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/səˈsteɪnəbl ɪˈkɒnəmi/</span> | nền kinh tế bền vững | build a **sustainable economy** |  |

| **native to**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈneɪtɪv tuː/</span> | có nguồn gốc từ nơi nào đó | [VP] **native** + to a place<br>originally **native to** | *This plant is native to Peru and when demand peaked, the price soared, making it unaffordable for local people.* |
| **organic bamboo**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɔːˈɡænɪk bæmˈbuː/</span> | tre hữu cơ | use / grow **organic bamboo**<br>certified **organic bamboo** | *Bamboo is one fabric we're all familiar with, but I didn't know that it was only organic bamboo[/ɔːˈɡænɪk bæmˈbuː/ : (noun) tre hữu cơ] that's truly sustainable.* |
| **ornamental plant**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌɔːnəˈmentl plɑːnt/</span> | cây cảnh | grow / consider an **ornamental plant**<br>beautiful **ornamental plant** | *Gardeners in the 19th century considered it a beautiful ornamental plant[/ˌɔːnəˈmentl plɑːnt/ : (noun) cây cảnh], which it is when it's kept under control.* |
| **Pará rubber tree**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈpærə ˈrʌbə triː/</span> | cây cao su Pará | grow / cultivate the **Pará rubber tree** | *One is natural rubber, which nearly all comes from the Pará rubber tree.* |
| **vegetation**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌvɛʤɪˈteɪʃən/</span> | thảm thực vật | clear / remove **vegetation**<br>dense / natural **vegetation** | *Some people believe that to prevent the predators from settling near the chickens, it's best to keep this area free from vegetation.* |
| **biological control**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌbaɪəˈlɒdʒɪkl kənˈtrəʊl/</span> | kiểm soát sinh học | use as / introduce for **biological control**<br>effective **biological control** | *Rhinella marina is a species of toad that was deliberately introduced from Hawaii in 1935 as a form of biological control[/ˌbaɪəˈlɒdʒɪkl kənˈtrəʊl/ : (noun) kiểm soát sinh học].* |
| **block out**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/blɒk aʊt/</span> | che khuất; ngăn lại | [VP] **block** + **out** sth<br>completely **block out** | *They're now seen as harmful because they block out[/blɒk aʊt/ : (verb) che khuất; ngăn lại] so much light that native wildflowers can't grow.* |
| **introduced species**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌɪntrəˈdjuːst ˈspiːʃiːz/</span> | loài được đưa vào môi trường mới | **introduce** / monitor **introduced species**<br>accidentally **introduced species** | *It's important to be clear that not all introduced species[/ˌɪntrəˈdjuːst ˈspiːʃiːz/ : (noun) loài được đưa vào môi trường mới] are invasive.* |
| **invasive lionfish**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪnˈveɪsɪv ˈlaɪənfɪʃ/</span> | cá sư tử xâm lấn | control / eradicate **invasive lionfish** | *What did you think of that article on invasive lionfish?* |
| **invasive species**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪnˈveɪsɪv ˈspiːʃiːz/</span> | loài xâm lấn | introduce / control **invasive species**<br>harmful **invasive species** | *Today I'm going to talk about invasive species[/ɪnˈveɪsɪv ˈspiːʃiːz/ : (noun) loài xâm lấn].* |
| **native species**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈneɪtɪv ˈspiːʃiːz/</span> | loài bản địa | threaten / protect **native species**<br>local **native species** | *First of all, invasive species may eat native species[/ˈneɪtɪv ˈspiːʃiːz/ : (noun) loài bản địa] or sometimes they may bring a disease with them.* |
| **non-native species**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/nɒn ˈneɪtɪv ˈspiːʃiːz/</span> | loài không bản địa | thrive as / spread **non-native species**<br>harmful **non-native species** | *Many introduced or non-native species[/nɒn ˈneɪtɪv ˈspiːʃiːz/ : (noun) loài không bản địa] thrive in new areas without posing any threat.* |
| **ocean biodiversity**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈəʊʃn ˌbaɪəʊdaɪˈvɜːsəti/</span> | đa dạng sinh học đại dương | study / protect **ocean biodiversity** | *Yeah, I learned a lot from her about ocean biodiversity.* |
| **survival**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/səˈvaɪvl/</span> | sự sống còn | threaten / ensure **survival**<br>long-term **survival** | *Species invasions are one of the biggest causes of damage in an ecosystem, actually putting its survival[/səˈvaɪvl/ : (noun) sự sống còn] at risk.* |
| **fungicide**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈfʌndʒɪsaɪd/</span> | thuốc diệt nấm | use / spray **fungicide**<br>chemical **fungicide** | *What about all the fungicides(cf. fungicide [/ˈfʌndʒɪsaɪd/ : (noun) thuốc diệt nấm]) and insecticides used in sheep farming?* |
| **insecticide**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈsektɪsaɪd/</span> | thuốc diệt côn trùng | use / apply **insecticide**<br>harmful **insecticide** | *What about all the fungicides and insecticides(cf. insecticide [/ˈsektɪsaɪd/ : (noun) thuốc diệt côn trùng]) used in sheep farming?* |
| **kennel**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈkenl/</span> | chuồng chó | use / check the **kennels**<br>on-board **kennel** | *The kennels(cf. kennel [/ˈkenl/ : (noun) chuồng chó]) on board are OK.* |
| **livestock**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈlaɪvstɒk/</span> | vật nuôi trang trại | raise / farm **livestock**<br>grazing **livestock** | *And I couldn't find any evidence for the claim about sheep farming being better for the environment than cattle farming (along with other livestock [/ˈlaɪvstɒk/ : (noun) vật nuôi trang trại]).* |
| **pest control**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/pest kənˈtrəʊl/</span> | kiểm soát sâu bệnh | use for / carry out **pest control**<br>biological **pest control** | *But sometimes humans deliberately move animals and plants around the world, for example, to use them to control pests(cf. pest control [/pest kənˈtrəʊl/ : (noun) kiểm soát sâu bệnh]).* |
| **rear**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/rɪə/</span> | nuôi dưỡng | [VP] **rear** + animals/chickens<br>successfully / traditionally **rear** | *As well as growing crops, small-scale farmers in the area also rear chickens.* |
| **shear sheep**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ʃɪə ʃiːp/</span> | xén lông cừu | [VP] **shear** + **sheep**<br>unethical to **shear sheep** | *I read different reports about how unethical it is to even shear sheep[/ʃɪə ʃiːp/ : (verb) xén lông cừu].* |
| **watch over**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈwɒʧ ˌəʊvə/</span> | trông chừng, giám sát | [VP] **watch over** sb/sth<br>carefully / constantly **watch over** | *During the breeding season, when the chickens were particularly vulnerable, farmers encouraged children to watch over the chickens to stop raptors trying to seize the young chicks.* |
| **wool production**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/wʊl prəˈdʌkʃn/</span> | sản xuất len | debate / increase **wool production**<br>large-scale **wool production** | *It was interesting to read about the debates surrounding wool production[/wʊl prəˈdʌkʃn/ : (noun) sản xuất len], and how ethical and sustainable that is.* |
| **mixed species**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/mɪkst ˈspiːʃiːz/</span> | Các loài khác nhau | **mixed species** woodland \ | *The woods are well established and varied, with an oak plantation and other areas of mixed species.* |
| **poplar tree**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈpɒplər triː/</span> | Cây dương | row of **poplar** **trees** \ | *We've been discussing what to do here, and we've decided to plant a poplar tree in the museum gardens.* |
| **paddocks**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈpædək/</span> | Khu vực đất (thường là khu chăn thả gia súc) | fenced **paddocks** \ | *It's thought that the Céide fields were mainly used as paddocks for animals to graze in.* |
| **system of rotational grazing**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈsɪstəm əv rəʊˈteɪʃənl ˈɡreɪzɪŋ/</span> | Hệ thống chăn thả luân phiên | use a **system of rotational grazing** \ | *They may have used a system of rotational grazing in order to prevent overgrazing and to allow for plant recovery and regrowth.* |
| **crop rotation system**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/krɒp rəʊˈteɪʃən ˈsɪstəm/</span> | Hệ thống luân canh cây trồng | adopt a **crop rotation system** \ | *The crop rotation system was partly responsible for this, as it would have been very intensive and was not sustainable.* |
| **intensive**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪnˈtensɪv/</span> | Khắc nghiệt, thâm canh cao độ | labour-**intensive** \ | *The crop rotation system was partly responsible for this, as it would have been very intensive and was not sustainable.* |
| **endangered species**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪnˈdeɪndʒəd ˈspiːʃiːz/</span> | loài có nguy cơ tuyệt chủng | protect **endangered species** | *A mix of species should always be planted, typical of the local natural forest ecosystem and including rare and endangered species, in order to create a rich ecosystem.* |
| **native forest species**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈneɪtɪv ˈfɒrɪst ˈspiːʃiːz/</span> | loài cây bản địa trong rừng | replant **native forest species** |  |
| **wildlife conservation**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈwaɪldlaɪf ˌkɒnsəˈveɪʃn/</span> | bảo tồn động vật hoang dã | fund **wildlife conservation** | *These could include selecting trees that can contribute to wildlife conservation, improve the availability of food for the local community, and maintain the stability of soil systems].* |
| **vulnerable species**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈvʌlnərəbl ˈspiːʃiːz/</span> | loài dễ bị tổn thương | protect **vulnerable species** | *Meeting as many of these goals as possible, whilst doing no harm to local communities, native ecosystems and vulnerable species, is the sign of a highly successful tree-planting scheme.* |
| **keystone species**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈkiːstəʊn ˈspiːʃiːz/</span> | loài chủ chốt | a **keystone species** | *Figs are a keystone species because of the critical role] they play in maintaining wildlife populations.* |
| **keystone role**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈkiːstəʊn rəʊl/</span> | vai trò chủ chốt | play a **keystone role** |  |
| **seed dispersers**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈsiːd dɪˈspɜːsə/</span> | tác nhân / loài phát tán hạt giống | act as **seed dispersers** | *They are central to tropical reforestation projects as they accelerate the speed of the recovery process by attracting animals and birds which act as natural seed dispersers.* |
| **fruit trees**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈfruːt triː/</span> | cây cho trái | plant **fruit trees** | *Unlike the majority of fruit trees, figs bear fruit all year round, providing a reliable food source for many species.* |
| **fig fruits**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈfɪɡ fruːt/</span> | quả sung | eat **fig fruits** | *At this site, for example, after only three rainy seasons, monkeys started visiting to eat the fig fruits, naturally dispersing seeds through defecation.* |
| **defecation**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌdefəˈkeɪʃn/</span> | sự bài tiết | seeds spread through **defecation** | *At this site, for example, after only three rainy seasons, monkeys started visiting to eat the fig fruits, naturally dispersing seeds through defecation.* |
| **aquatic species**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/əˈkwætɪk ˈspiːʃiːz/</span> | loài thủy sinh | protect **aquatic species** |  |

<a id="section-24"></a>
## 24. ACADEMIC & ABSTRACT LANGUAGE
*117 words*

| Word | Meaning | Collocation | Example |
|------|---------|-------------|---------|
| **byproduct**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈbaɪprɒdʌkt/</span> | sản phẩm phụ | made from / create a **byproduct**<br>unwanted **byproduct** | *Made from byproducts(cf. byproduct [/ˈbaɪprɒdʌkt/ : (noun) sản phẩm phụ]) of the cotton industry to create a kind of vegan silk.* |
| **consequences**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈkɒnsɪkwəns/</span> | hậu quả | have / face **consequences**<br>serious **consequences** | *As you can see, there are serious consequences for designs which don't consider the needs of all users.* |
| **devastation**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌdevəˈsteɪʃn/</span> | sự tàn phá | cause / witness **devastation**<br>widespread **devastation** | *The most dangerous threat is a fungus, which destroyed large numbers of rubber trees in Brazil, and which could cause devastation to plantations worldwide.* |
| **disturbed**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/dɪˈstɜːb/</span> | làm xáo trộn | negatively / easily **disturbed**<br>routine **disturbed** | *They may also be negatively affected by having their routine disturbed.* |
| **have an impact on**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/hæv ən ˈɪmpækt ɒn/</span> | có ảnh hưởng đến | [VP] **have** + an **impact** on sth<br>huge / significant **impact** on | *It's worth looking at the problems non-inclusive designs cause when not enough consideration is given to a range of users, as it can have an impact on people's lives and prove highly problematic.* |
| **implication**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌɪmplɪˈkeɪʃn/</span> | hệ quả; hàm ý | consider / understand the **implications**<br>serious / wider **implications** | *It wasn't exactly a feel-good conclusion, because it's hard to see any real solution for a lot of the problems (cf. implication).* |
| **limiting factor**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈlɪmɪtɪŋ ˈfæktə/</span> | yếu tố hạn chế | become a / identify a **limiting factor**<br>major **limiting factor** | *So what exactly is limiting the supply(cf. limiting factor) of natural rubber?* |
| **pose a threat**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/pəʊz ə θret/</span> | gây ra mối đe dọa | [VP] **pose** + a **threat** to sth<br>potentially / seriously **pose a threat** | *Many introduced or non-native species thrive in new areas without posing any threat(cf. pose a threat [/pəʊz ə θret/ : (verb) gây ra mối đe dọa]).* |
| **pose a threat to**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/pəʊz ə θrɛt tuː/</span> | gây mối đe dọa cho | [VP] **pose a threat to** sb/sth<br>serious / major **threat** | *And local farmers also pose a threat to these birds.* |
| **put down to**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/pʊt daʊn tuː/</span> | quy cho, cho là do | [VP] **put** + sth **down** to sth<br>often / largely **put down to** | *True, but people are relying on digital devices, which changes habits like note-taking and makes their punctuation completely inconsistent, which you can put down to lack of practice.* |
| **source of**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/sɔːs əv/</span> | nguồn của | [VP] a major / main **source of**<br>**source of** income / of food | *For example, a lot of damage can be caused to farmers' crops by rodents, such as rats, which are a major source of food for raptors.* |
| **dramatic change**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/drəˈmætɪk ʧeɪndʒ/</span> | sự thay đổi lớn | undergo / see a **dramatic change**<br>sudden **dramatic change** | *I reckon the most important thing is the dramatic change they underwent during World War II.* |
| **minimise**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈmɪnɪmaɪz/</span> | giảm thiểu | [VP] **minimise** + sth<br>help **minimise** | *Well, tell the council, because they may be able to give you some advice on how to minimise it.* |
| **outdated**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌaʊtˈdeɪtɪd/</span> | lỗi thời | badly / now **outdated**<br>**outdated** information / equipment | *I watched to the end, but you're right, it was showing its age (cf. outdated).* |
| **recent changes**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈriːsnt tʃeɪndʒ/</span> | thay đổi gần đây | notice / make **recent changes**<br>significant **recent changes** | *And what about recent changes?* |
| **revolutionised**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌrɛvəˈluːʃənaɪzd/</span> | cách mạng hóa | [VP] **revolutionise** + sth<br>completely **revolutionised** | *Its invention revolutionised the pottery industry, allowing multiple items to be produced in a day.* |
| **speed up**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/spiːd ʌp/</span> | tăng tốc | [VP] **speed** + up<br>gradually **speed up** | *It's more important to focus on accuracy and as they get more confident, I think they eventually speed up.* |
| **thrive**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/θraɪv/</span> | phát triển mạnh | [VP] **thrive** + in sth<br>continue to / manage to **thrive** | *Many introduced or non-native species thrive[/θraɪv/ : (verb) phát triển mạnh] in new areas without posing any threat.* |
| **underwent**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌʌndəˈɡəʊ /</span> | trải qua | [VP] undergo + sth<br>recently **underwent** | *I reckon the most important thing is the dramatic change they underwent during World War II.* |
| **associate**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/əˈsəʊsieɪt ˈsʌmθɪŋ wɪð/</span> | liên hệ cái gì với cái gì | [VP] **associate** + sth with sth<br>closely / strongly **associate** | *Marketing surveys have shown that a significant number of shoppers now associate Norway with excellent seafood.* |
| **distinct**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/dɪˈdɪŋkt/</span> | riêng biệt; khác rõ | quite / entirely **distinct**<br>**distinct** difference / advantage | *Most people use these terms interchangeably, but in fact, the difference is quite distinct[/dɪˈstɪŋkt/ : (adj) riêng biệt; khác rõ] when you think about it.* |
| **distinguish**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/dɪˈstɪŋɡwɪʃ/</span> | phân biệt | [VP] **distinguish** + sth from sth<br>help **distinguish** | *You need to play lots of games to help them distinguish letter shapes.* |
| **had an advantage over**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/hæv ən ədˈvɑːntɪdʒ ˈəʊvə/</span> | có lợi thế hơn ai/điều gì | [VP] have + an **advantage** over sb/sth<br>clear / significant **advantage** over | *Oat milk had an advantage over other alternative milk products, such as almond milk, because it requires significantly less water and has a relatively low carbon footprint.* |
| **in relation to**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪn rɪˈlênʃən tuː/</span> | liên quan tới | [VP] in **relation** + to sth<br>directly **in relation to** | *I thought spatial awareness was more to do with knowing where you are in relation to objects or other people.* |
| **inconsistent**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪnkənˈsɪstənt/</span> | không nhất quán | highly / remarkably **inconsistent**<br>**inconsistent** results / behaviour | *True, but people are relying on digital devices, which changes habits like note-taking and makes their punctuation completely inconsistent, which you can put down to lack of practice.* |
| **interchangeably**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌɪntəˈtʃeɪndʒəbli/</span> | có thể thay thế cho nhau | use terms **interchangeably**<br>often / commonly **interchangeably** | *Most people use these terms interchangeably[/ˌɪntəˈtʃeɪndʒəbli/ : (adv) có thể thay thế cho nhau], but in fact, the difference is quite distinct when you think about it.* |
| **resemble**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/rɪˈzɛmbl/</span> | giống | [VP] **resemble** + sth<br>closely / strongly **resemble** | *Wasn't it in the 19th century that theatre programmes started to resemble programmes today?* |
| **assess all the available evidence**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/əˈses ˈevɪdəns/</span> | đánh giá bằng chứng | [VP] **assess** + the **available** **evidence**<br>carefully / thoroughly **assess** | *Earlier this year, a research team set out to assess all the available evidence so that they could highlight the potential for music in surgical recovery.* |
| **classic example**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈklæsɪk ɪɡˈzɑːmpl/</span> | ví dụ điển hình | a **classic example** of<br>cite / give a **classic example** | *Quinoa is a classic example.* |
| **controversial**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌɒntrəˈvɜːʃəl/</span> | gây tranh cãi | highly / deeply **controversial**<br>**controversial** decision / issue | *It is very effective at engaging the audience, even though some reviews were controversial.* |
| **creative suggestions**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/kriˈeɪtɪv səˈdʒestʃən/</span> | đề xuất sáng tạo | come up with / offer **creative suggestions**<br>original **creative suggestions** | *Are we meant to come up with creative suggestions for these houses?* |

| **express frustration**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪkˈsprɛs frʌˈstreɪʃən/</span> | bày tỏ sự thất vọng | [VP] **express frustration** + about / over sth | *There's no available diagnosis or medical cure, and people often express frustration about it.* |
| **frightened**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈfraɪtnd/</span> | sợ hãi | get / become **frightened**<br>easily **frightened** | *We don't want any accidents or anyone getting frightened.* |
| **grateful for**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈɡreɪtfʊl fə/</span> | biết ơn về | be / feel **grateful for** | *Tourists often ask how to get to a particular shop and they might also be grateful for recommendations about what to visit.* |
| **impressed**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪmˈprɛst/</span> | ấn tượng | really / deeply **impressed**<br>**impressed** by / with | *I've only been there a couple of times, but I was really impressed.* |

| **isolated**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈaɪsəleɪtɪd/</span> | bị cô lập | feel / become **isolated**<br>socially **isolated** | *In my case, far from making me feel isolated, it actually does the opposite.* |

| **regretful**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/rɪˈɡrɛtfəl/</span> | hối tiếc | deeply / genuinely **regretful**<br>**regretful** tone / feeling | *I can't help feeling regretful that fewer people write by hand.* |
| **relieved**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/rɪˈliːvd/</span> | nhẹ nhõm | feel / be **relieved** | *But you must be relieved to have more time to care for your mother.* |

| **concentration**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌkɒnsənˈtreɪʃən/</span> | sự tập trung | develop / improve **concentration**<br>require **concentration** | *What's less obvious is how it helps develop their concentration.* |

| **adapt**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/əˈdæpt/</span> | Thích nghi, điều chỉnh cho phù hợp | **adapt** to change \ | *We can organize a wide range of activities and adapt them to suit all ages.* |

| **formation**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/fɔːˈmeɪʃən/</span> | sự hình thành | letter **formation**<br>support with **formation** | *They need so much more support with letter formation.* |
| **handwriting**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈhændˌraɪtɪŋ/</span> | chữ viết tay | improve / practise **handwriting**<br>neat / poor **handwriting** | *How are you getting on with the assignment on handwriting?* |
| **illiterate**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪˈlɪtərət/</span> | mù chữ | largely / completely **illiterate**<br>**illiterate** population | *I also think it's important to mention that lots of ordinary people at that time were illiterate, so theatre programmes were of limited value in advertising plays.* |
| **joined up**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ʤɔɪn ʌp/</span> | nối liền (chữ viết) | **joined up** writing / letters<br>become **joined up** | *Did you read that article on the benefits of teaching print writing rather than cursive handwriting, where the letters are joined up?* |
| **legibility**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/lɪˈdʒɪbɪləti/</span> | tính dễ đọc | improve **legibility**<br>poor **legibility** | *That's more important for legibility than trying to get them to write in a straight line.* |
| **monthly magazine**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈmʌnθli ˌmæɡəˈziːn/</span> | tạp chí hàng tháng | produce / publish a **monthly magazine** | *The town produces a monthly magazine and anyone who lives in the town can send in articles.* |
| **print writing**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/prɪnt ˈraɪtɪŋ/</span> | chữ in thường | teach **print writing**<br>switch to **print writing** | *Did you read that article on the benefits of teaching print writing rather than cursive handwriting, where the letters are joined up?* |
| **publish details**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈpʌblɪʃ ˈdiːteɪlz/</span> | công bố thông tin chi tiết | [VP] **publish** + **details**<br>refuse to / never **publish details** | *We don't actually publish your name or details.* |
| **punctuation**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌpʌŋktʃuˈeɪʃən/</span> | dấu câu | inconsistent / correct **punctuation**<br>use **punctuation** | *True, but people are relying on digital devices, which changes habits like note-taking and makes their punctuation completely inconsistent, which you can put down to lack of practice.* |
| **script**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/skrɪpt/</span> | bản thảo, chữ viết | read / decipher the **script**<br>illegible **script** | *Marks are definitely affected if examiners can't read the script, impacting their overall academic performance.* |
| **accused the theatre of**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/əˈkjuːz ˈsʌmwʌn əv ˈsʌmθɪŋ/</span> | buộc tội ai đó điều gì | [VP] **accuse** + sb of sth<br>publicly **accused** | *And if that didn't happen, people accused the theatre of breaking their agreement with the audience.* |
| **breaking their agreement with**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/breɪk wʌnz əˈɡriːmənt wɪð/</span> | phá vỡ thỏa thuận với ai | [VP] **break** + an **agreement** with sb<br>allegedly **breaking** **agreement** | *And if that didn't happen, people accused the theatre of breaking their agreement with the audience.* |
| **in favour of**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪn ˈfeɪvər əv/</span> | ủng hộ | [VP] in **favour** + of sth<br>strongly / clearly **in favour of** | *In the USA, the brand decided against a big retail launch in favour of getting the product into coffee chains, which removed the need for a big advertising budget.* |
| **terminology**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌtɜːmɪˈnɒlədʒi/</span> | thuật ngữ chuyên môn | explain / understand **terminology**<br>technical / sailing **terminology** | *Yes, everything's included (cf. all inclusive) and you also get a really good dictionary explaining all the sailing terminology.* |

<a id="section-27"></a>
## 27. TIME, QUANTITY & MEASUREMENT
*14 words*

| Word | Meaning | Collocation | Example |
|------|---------|-------------|---------|
| **annually**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈænjuəli/</span> | hằng năm | performed **annually**<br>held **annually** | *Now, most people undergo a surgical procedure at some point in their lives and more than 51 million operations are performed annually in the USA.* |
| **at short notice**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ət ʃɔːt ˈnəʊtɪs/</span> | thông báo gấp, thời gian gấp | available / arranged **at short notice** | *We try to use volunteers who are flexible though because some festival events are held outdoors and the weather may affect the size of the audience and even whether the event can take place, so there can be changes at short notice.* |
| **deadline**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈdedlaɪn/</span> | hạn chót | meet / miss a **deadline**<br>tight **deadline** | *When's the deadline?* |
| **outlive**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌaʊtˈlɪv/</span> | sống lâu hơn, tồn tại lâu hơn | [VP] **outlive** + sb/sth<br>**outlive** its usefulness / a generation | *Like our ancestors, they hope that something they create will also outlive them.* |
| **stands the test of time**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/stænd ðə tɛst əv taɪm/</span> | tồn tại lâu dài | truly / certainly **stands the test of time** | *And we know this because it stands the test of time.* |
| **swift pace**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/swɪft peɪs/</span> | tốc độ nhanh | keep up a / maintain a **swift pace** | *If there are dozens of extras involved, you'll need to keep up a swift pace and not spend too long on each person.* |
| **comprises**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/kəmˈpraɪz/</span> | bao gồm | [VP] comprise + sth<br>now **comprises** | *It already comprises over 200,000 programmes, which is amazing!* |
| **excessive numbers**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ɪkˈsesɪv ˈnʌmbərz/</span> | số lượng quá mức | involve / attract **excessive numbers** | *Overtourism occurs in places where excessive numbers of tourists cause significant problems.* |
| **insufficient**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌɪnsəˈfɪʃnt/</span> | không đủ | wholly / increasingly **insufficient**<br>**insufficient** supply / evidence | *Recently, however, concern's been growing that supplies may soon be insufficient for the world's needs.* |
| **average-sized**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈævərɪʤ saɪzd/</span> | kích cỡ trung bình | **average-sized** male / adult<br>an **average-sized** | *Inexplicably, most cars are still crash-tested using a dummy based on an average-sized male.* |
| **massive**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˈmæsɪv/</span> | khổng lồ, rất lớn | **massive** increase / development | *They've opened some massive new shopping centres / retail centres outside my city.* |
| **worldwide**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ˌwɜːldˈwaɪd/</span> | trên toàn thế giới | known / famous **worldwide**<br>**worldwide** success / recognition | *He'll be telling you how it all happened, and how it changed his life, and in a remarkably short time, he had opened an amazing 76 restaurants worldwide.* |
| **take place**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/teɪk pleɪs/</span> | diễn ra | [VP] **take place** + outdoors<br>events **take place** | *We try to use volunteers who are flexible though because some festival events are held outdoors and the weather may affect the size of the audience and even whether the event can take place, so there can be changes at short notice.* |
| **the birth of**<br><span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.875em;">/ðə bɜːθ əv/</span> | sự ra đời của | since **the birth of** | *Interest in food fashions has risen rapidly since the birth of the smartphone, when people first began taking photos of their food and instantly sharing them with their friends.* |
